data-score=\"13\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"13\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-14-1\" data-question=\"13,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. scope</p><p><strong>What does the contradiction between the wrapper and the catalogue initially justify?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. Concluding that every academy record is false.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. Accusing Bellamy of putting the candy inside.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. Checking the record of when the case was opened.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. Discarding the wrapper as worthless.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"12\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"14\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c03","title":"A Sentence That Wasn't Hers","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0015","paragraphs":["At eight the next morning, Mara was standing outside the workshop with two coffees when Lucien Orr came down the steps carrying her report.","\"I thought you should see this before everyone else does,\" he said.","She recognized her title, her numbered photographs, and the small stain she had made with a wet thumb. Then she reached the final paragraph. According to the document, she had established beyond reasonable doubt that the academy had deliberately falsified its collection records. The words were graceful, confident, and absolutely not hers.","\"Where did you get it?\"","\"The academic review portal. It was circulated to the faculty before dawn. You appear to have attached a page from an unpublished research paper.\"","A line beneath the paragraph identified the source as a doctoral thesis. Mara read the allegation again. It was almost admirably constructed: a stolen sentence making an accusation she could not yet prove. Anyone looking only at the last page would see an ignorant newcomer using borrowed authority to condemn an institution.","She handed him a coffee. \"Tell me exactly what you downloaded.\"","Lucien accepted it, surprised enough to become useful. He had received an automatic notification at six. The report was attached to the message. He had saved it locally before entering the portal, where the same text appeared. He had not opened the source paper. He had not sent the report to anyone else.","\"You can say that in front of the dean?\"","\"I came to warn you, not to join your defense.\"","\"Then say only what you know. It may achieve both.\"","The dean's office overlooked a memorial garden. Below the window, a workman was cutting dead branches from an ancient pear tree. Mara concentrated on the moving blades while Dean Sayer asked whether she understood the consequences of academic misconduct. She did. What she did not understand was why her file had changed after she submitted it.","Sayer pushed a printed page across the desk. \"Your password is your responsibility.\"","\"So is the academy's record of what that password was used to do. I want the original submission preserved.\"","\"The system is operational.\"","\"That isn't the same as saying nobody changed the file.\"","Holt, seated beside the cabinet, lifted her head. Cassian stood by the door, still wearing the assistant's apron. He had arrived after a message from the registrar and had offered no explanation of his status. To the dean he was a quiet man who had witnessed the work. Mara wondered how long he would allow that impression to stand.","\"We use cloud computing,\" Sayer said. \"The report is on a secure server.\"","\"Then there may be a version history.\"","\"You specialize in mechanisms, Miss Vale.\"","\"I do. Which is why I would like someone who specializes in your server to evaluate it.\"","Sayer looked at Holt. She said, \"That is a reasonable request. If we can calculate an applicant's score, we can preserve the material on which it is based.\"","The dean agreed to call the information bureau. The pause did not mean Mara had been cleared. She was permitted to continue preliminary work under supervision, but her access to the research wing remained blocked. A formal meeting would follow once the original file and its associated records were available. It was a limited outcome, better than immediate expulsion and worse than the morning she had expected.","Outside, Cassian reached for the coffee she was still holding. It had gone cold. \"You could have told him who I am.\"","\"Would it have changed the version history?\"","\"It would have changed his tone.\"","\"I need his evidence. You can save his tone for a special occasion.\"","Cassian considered this. \"My list is becoming long.\"","She almost laughed. The warmth helped more than he could know. It also made her want to lean against him, which seemed dangerous while her hands were trembling. She held the cup with both of them instead.","In the workshop, the other contestants had already heard. The teacher from the charter school greeted her normally. The school leaver stared, then pretended to study a cube of cracked glass. Bellamy examined her bench with the attentive expression of someone finding a stain on a valuable carpet.","\"A pity,\" he said. \"Talent without discipline rarely lasts.\"","Mara set down her bag. \"I would prefer not to be judged on a paragraph I didn't write.\"","\"The familiar claim of every candidate who gets caught.\"","\"Also a possible statement of fact.\"","He moved on before she could ask what would distinguish the two. She wanted to chase him. Instead, she read the final paragraph again, marking every detail it contained. Whoever had added it knew that she had found the discrepancy. Whoever had chosen the source wanted the allegation to resemble plagiarism. Those might be the same person, but she had no justification for treating that assumption as proof.","Holt called the group together. The day's instruction concerned the interpretation of experimental results. Against the background of the morning's uproar, the subject felt pointed. Holt did not mention Mara's report. She put two repaired springs and a small cylinder on the table.","\"A spring holds under forty tests,\" she said. \"Does that mean it will never fail?\"","The school leaver shook his head.","\"It breaks on the next test. Does that mean the first forty results were false?\"","Lucien folded his arms. \"It means the variable changed, or our model was incomplete.\"","\"Or,\" Ada added, \"the thing got tired.\"","\"A less formal description, but not necessarily an irrational one. Now consider whether speed, load, and temperature were held constant.\"","Mara understood the point without needing it directed at her. She could regard the system as flawed without assuming every person who used it was dishonest. She could also comply with a rule while asking who had failed to abide by it. The harder task was to guard against the comfort of a complete explanation when she had only a fragment.","At the end of the demonstration, Ada slid a folded note beside Mara's elbow. The theater copy of the catalogue had been moved to an off-site museum store. She had called the retired manager who kept the old keys. He could let them in that evening if they brought identification. The store was north of the city, and the last bus left before sunset.","\"We can take a taxi,\" Cassian said.","Mara looked at him.","\"I understand. No carriage with a crest. No escort. No threatening the manager.\"","\"I was going to ask whether you had money.\"","\"A challenging question from a woman who still has my key.\"","She touched the inside pocket of her coat. \"An unsecured asset. You ought to be more careful.\"","The store was a desolate brick building behind a former tennis club. Its manager, Mr. Fenwick, wore jeans and a Swiss watch that had stopped. He had spent thirty years moving artifacts from one underfunded public collection to another. His learned opinion was that the city's greatest talent lay in renaming a net loss as an adjustment.","He led them past a marble king with a broken nose, a violin in a sealed case, and a richly colored map of North Africa. The collection was a reminder that objects could outlive the people who explained them. Without the manual describing an old machine, a perfectly ordinary part could become a puzzle, a weapon, or a myth.","Fenwick opened a cabinet. \"You wanted the second catalogue.\"","Mara's breath caught. \"How did you know there were two?\"","\"Because I carried them here. One for the audience, one for accounting. These people used to be less imaginative about their filing.\"","The public catalogue described gifts, performances, and school visits. The other recorded loans of equipment. Its pages were arranged by recipient number. Most names had been removed with a neat strip of white tape. Page eighteen had been torn out, leaving a jagged edge.","Mara checked the neighboring entries. The missing page had covered a series of demonstrations in buildings outside the academy, including a hospital and a theater. She could not tell what had been demonstrated. A note in the margin referred to consent forms stored separately. Another mentioned \"verbal recall after exposure.\" The phrase made the room feel colder.","Cassian stood completely still beside her.","\"It could be an ordinary cognitive experiment,\" Mara said, more to him than to herself.","\"It could.\"","\"We need the forms.\"","\"And someone qualified to interpret them.\"","She nodded. Noor, perhaps, once they had permission to obtain copies. The reference did not establish a connection to Mnemosyne. It gave them a reason to look for one. Mara wrote down the shelf number, then asked Fenwick to record that she had examined the book in his presence.","He agreed, but his hand paused over the date. \"A man asked for this last month. Nervous fellow. Said he wanted to correct a mistake.\"","Mara kept her voice level. \"Did he leave a name?\"","\"No. He left something in the back. I was supposed to give it to whoever asked the same question.\"","Inside the cover was a small envelope. Its contents were not a letter but a photograph of a workbench. She handed Mara the food before asking to see the photograph.","\"Eat. Then tell me which parts you know, which parts you suspect, and which parts Cassian has been brooding about.\"","\"His contribution is integral to the atmosphere.\"","\"I brought a second sandwich for that.\"","They sat in the campus garden beneath a sculpture of a woman holding a lantern. From the far side of a brick wall came the sounds of the academy's junior debate club: someone was making an aggressive case for abolishing fees, while someone else shouted that the exchequer was not a bottomless vacuum. Noor waited until Mara had finished describing the altered report and the second catalogue.","\"A cognitive study isn't automatically sinister,\" she said. \"Neither is a participant number. Researchers use them to keep names out of working documents. What matters is what the participants were told, what they agreed to, and what was actually done.\"","\"The public description and the accounting book don't match.\"","\"Then clarify the contradiction. Don't fill the gap with the worst thing you can imagine.\"","Mara turned the photograph over. The missing witness had left no greeting, no explanation, and no assurance that entering Room Seventeen would be safe. She could see a coil of cable on the bench and a stripe of pale paint across its leg. The number beneath it was the only feature that distinguished an ordinary work surface from a possible hiding place.","Noor held it near the light. \"Do you have permission to inspect that room?\"","\"I have an application, a pending evaluation, and a damaged reputation.\"","\"A series of opportunities to practice patience.\"","\"I'm remarkably well-educated by that measure.\"","The laboratory in the west wing had once belonged to a research institute affiliated with the academy. Its work had included the restoration of old sound recordings, optical imaging, and experimental tests of verbal memory. The institute had closed two years earlier. Rooms were now being used for storage while a consultancy prepared an appraisal of the remaining equipment. That much was visible in public records. The list of participants was not.","Noor had asked a biostatistician to consider the information they could lawfully share. His tentative opinion was modest: a catalogue could identify the date and setting of an experiment, but it could not establish its results. A statistic without a description of how it was produced might be accurate and still be misleading. He could compute a figure from a table, not the reliability of a process whose intermediate steps were missing.","Mara folded her arms. \"Does every researcher say 'it depends' before breakfast?\"","\"Only the responsible ones. It doesn't mean we stop. It means we decide what to look at next.\"","What they needed next was access. Mara drafted a request for a supervised inspection of the workbench, with the photograph attached as an appendix. She did not accuse the academy of corruption. She asked it to preserve an object connected to an ongoing missing-person inquiry. Cassian supplied the inquiry reference and signed as an investigating officer. It was the first time his professional role had entered her fellowship paperwork.","\"Will that give you an advantage?\" Noor asked him.","\"It gives the request a basis. I won't supervise the inspection or the judging.\"","Mara appreciated the distinction. She also realized how much she had wanted to make everything happen through her own ability. Accepting necessary help was different from letting someone purchase a favorable result. She had to unlearn the habit of treating both as a loss.","Dean Sayer received them shortly before noon. The campus was abuzz with preparations for the donor ceremony. Beyond his window, a gardener chased a rabbit away from a bed of seedlings. Sayer looked as though he would have preferred to be dealing with the rabbit.","\"Your request puts us in a difficult position,\" he said.","\"The missing witness is in a worse one,\" Mara replied.","Cassian touched her wrist, lightly enough for her to ignore. She took a breath. \"I understand that you have procedures. Tell me what independent supervision would make the inspection possible.\"","The dean agreed to a limited visit after consulting the academy's security officer. No equipment could be activated, no papers removed, and no locked container opened without additional approval. Holt would accompany them. Bellamy would identify the collection items. The arrangement was imperfect, but it was not an invitation to wait indefinitely.","\"And my report?\" Mara asked.","An initial examination showed that a processing service had touched her file after submission. The service had been introduced to add catalogue references automatically. It had access to the body of a report as well as its attachments. Sayer said the system was being reviewed.","\"So the alteration wasn't necessarily accidental,\" she said.","\"Nor have we established that it was deliberate.\"","\"I agree. Please preserve both possibilities in the notice to the judges.\"","He seemed surprised to find her cautious. Perhaps, she thought, he had mistaken anger for an inability to reason. Perhaps she had made the same mistake about him.","At two, they assembled outside Room Seventeen. Mara could hear applause from the auditorium through an open window. A big-name actress was presenting a restoration award to a benefactor. The words \"future\" and \"humanity\" emerged clearly from the microphone. The sentences between them were lost in the wind.","Bellamy unlocked the door. \"Nothing here is of significant value.\"","\"Then the inspection should be painless,\" Holt said.","The room smelled of paper and warm metal. Scattered cases lined the walls. An unfashionable stereo stood beside an optical reader, and a cardboard ox looked down from a shelf of discarded teaching models. Mara resisted the inclination to examine everything. They had come for the bench, and the permission had a limit.","There it was, under the opposite window. Pale stripe. Missing drawer handle. Four metal feet.","\"May I look underneath?\" she asked.","The security officer nodded. Mara knelt. Cassian remained at the threshold, keeping away from the object so nobody could question what he had touched. It was a small precaution. She saw what it cost him when a loose screw scratched her finger and he made an abrupt movement before stopping himself.","The number seventeen was gone. Someone had sanded the underside of the bench. Mara took photographs from several angles, then pointed to a patch of grain that differed from the wood around it.","\"Recent work?\" Holt asked.","\"Possibly. I can't date it by sight. But the paper in the photograph was caught under that screw. There's still something there.\"","The officer used a small knife to lift the edge. A narrow strip of paper remained beneath the washer, protected from the sanding. It bore three printed words and part of a line: \"FOLLOW-UP / TRANSPORT / DISTRICT.\" Below them, in blue ink, was a curved mark that might have been a letter or nothing more than a pen test.","Mara felt hope rise, then forced herself to lower its speed. A fragment was a fragment. Her mother had taught her that people could ruin an object by being too eager to restore what they thought ought to be there.","\"Can you photograph both sides before you move it further?\" she asked. \"And the screw in place.\"","Holt gave her an approving glance. Bellamy's indifference had become very still.","Inside the drawer lay an envelope containing a purchase receipt for replacement fittings. The buyer was the academy. The seller was a subsidiary of Ardent's equipment company. The receipt was dated seventeen days earlier, three days after the witness had last been seen. The sequence was significant. It did not prove who had sanded the bench, or why.","The officer sealed the paper strip and receipt separately. Mara signed the record as a witness. Bellamy signed next. His pen paused when he reached the description of the missing number.","\"That photograph could be older than you think,\" he said.","\"Then the date is something we should look into,\" Mara replied.","They emerged as the ceremony ended. The actress stood beside a red automobile, smiling for a photographer. Above the entrance, the academy's banner declared that learning enlarged the horizon. Mara wondered whether that promise extended to people whose questions made its patrons uncomfortable.","Noor was waiting at the gate. She examined Mara's finger, cleaned the cut, and added a plaster before anyone could turn it into a heroic wound. \"You have evidence of a paper and a recent purchase,\" she said. \"Keep them separate from the explanation you prefer.\"","\"You make mystery less glamorous.\"","\"And more likely to survive contact with a judge.\"","Later, Cassian walked with Mara to the bus stop. The afternoon mist had begun to settle over the harbor. She was worn out, hungry again, and anxious about the competition. Whatever she found in the old wing, someone had tried to make her words unreliable. They could try again.","\"You kept your promise,\" she said.","\"Which one?\"","\"You stayed out of the way.\"","\"It was a difficult technical assignment.\"","She laughed, then offered him her uninjured hand. \"You can help with this part.\"","He took it without pulling her closer. For a moment they stood beside the timetable like any ordinary couple deciding which bus to catch. The ease of it unsettled her more than the cut had.","Her smartphone vibrated. A message from Holt appeared on the screen: the preliminary round would go ahead the following morning. Mara's original report would be assessed separately from the disputed final paragraph. Until the review ended, every stage of her work would be recorded.","A second message followed from an unknown number.","Withdraw, and the missing page stays missing.","Mara showed Cassian. He read it once, then looked toward the academy, where the last guests were leaving beneath the banner about learning.","\"Do you want to stop?\" he asked.","She put the phone away. \"I want a better The chairman of the fellowship board had turned sixty that morning, and somebody had decided that the occasion justified stopping work while he cut a slice on the stage. Mara watched the cream slide slowly towards a priceless carpet and wondered why the academy considered her the principal threat to its collection.","\"Pay attention to the cake,\" she whispered to Cassian. \"We may need an unbiased witness.\"","\"To the chairman's age?\"","\"To the first casualty.\"","He passed her a clean towel. She caught the falling cream before it reached the floor. The chairman thanked her with a mouthful of sponge, then introduced the competition as a triumph of education over inherited advantage. Cassian studied the program with admirable restraint.","Behind the chairman stood a screen displaying the academy's postwar history. A teacher had founded its public workshop in a butcher's former store, repairing objects for families who could not afford private treatment. The place had grown from a room into an acre of laboratories and classrooms. Its first charter promised that expertise would serve the public. Mara hoped the institution still remembered why the promise had been made in the first place.","The independent entrants were introduced with the established students. The middle-class mother who had taught at a charter school stood beside a graduate student sponsored by a global design brand. A restorer who had chosen to immigrate from a tropical island had brought his grandmother's tools. Demographic differences, the chairman said, enriched the learning community. Mara noticed that the camera remained on the students with the most expensive clothes.","Their assignment was straightforward. Each entrant would demonstrate a preliminary intervention, explain its limitations, and defend a revised plan. The ongoing investigation into Mara's report would not affect the score unless the disputed text entered her presentation. She had prepared a condition sheet without it and given copies to Holt and the independent overseer. Her purpose was to show that removing the old candy had allowed the ticket mechanism to move without causing further wear-and-tear.","As yet, she had not repaired the damaged screen. That would require an analysis of its fiber, which could not be completed during an afternoon excursion through the supply cupboard. She had written that the mechanism might run safely under a low, controlled load. The word \"might\" had acquired a red border on her note pad.","Lucien gave an outstanding demonstration of a silver clock. He was able to explain the equation behind its timing without losing the general public, a skill Mara respected. When a child asked why the clock did not get bored, he considered the question fairly and replied that only its owner appeared susceptible to that problem. There was more humor in him than she had expected.","Then her number appeared. Cassian lifted the small theater onto the table. He placed the base carefully, checked that the power was disconnected, and stepped back. His task was to bring the object and remain available for safety instructions. Mara was the holder of the entry, the expertise, and whatever consequences followed.","She explained the candy and the conflicting records. She did not bring up the missing page. The preliminary demonstration had to satisfy a narrow question; adding an unproved theory about the academy would give the judges a legitimate reason to stop her. On the screen, her photographs showed exactly what she had removed and how she intended to preserve it.","The first cycle went smoothly. A painted monkey crossed the little stage, bowing to a paper audience. A woman in blue followed, carrying a basket. The host smiled. Mara let herself relish the sound of the mechanism turning without a scrape.","On the second cycle, the lamp brightened. The figures began to move twice as fast.","Her eyes went to the control box. The speed setting had shifted. Cassian's hand moved towards the switch, but he waited for her instruction.","\"Disconnect it. Now.\"","The mechanism stopped with the monkey halfway through its bow. A fine crack had appeared at the edge of the screen. Mara felt a cascade of dread, followed by the colder knowledge that she had only a few seconds before someone supplied an explanation for her.","\"A setback,\" said the host. \"Would you like to try again?\"","She looked at the little figure, trapped in a posture of absurd politeness. \"Not until I understand why the setting changed. Repeating the same action would not make it safe.\"","The chairman leaned forward. \"You may have exceeded the permitted load.\"","\"That is one possibility. A fault in the controller is another. I would like both recorded.\"","\"Your assessment mentioned a safe operating range.\"","\"A proposed range under specified conditions. Those conditions changed.\"","She could hear resentment in her own voice and forced it down. Being angry was not the same as being wrong, but it could make her unnecessarily imprecise. The radical thing to do now was not to defend every sentence she had written. It was to preserve the difference between an idea and a finding.","Holt asked the official technician to inspect the settings. He found the display reading twenty-four rather than twelve. Nobody could say from the screen alone whether Mara had entered the wrong number or the system had replaced it. The board's first draft of the rules had required an external controller. Its revised version permitted the academy's shared interface. Her object was the first of the day to use it.","\"Then we can optimize the input,\" the technician said. \"The program is designed to learn from repeated use.\"","\"Please don't let it learn by destroying this,\" Mara replied. \"I'd rather use an isolated controller for a test.\"","The chairman called the request disproportionate. The device had worked for other projects. A board member compared her objection to refusing a public bus because one passenger had once fallen over. The analogy drew a few smiles.","Mara placed her original condition sheet beside the control box. \"If a bus begins to accelerate without the driver asking it to, I would like the driver to stop. I would not call that an abdication of responsibility.\"","The smiles changed direction. She wished she had not enjoyed it quite so much.","An official from the city regulator asked whether a recording showed the original setting. Cassian pointed to the fixed camera but did not interpret it. Its bandwidth had dropped before the demonstration, leaving the digits blurred. His own recording was clear enough to show Mara's hands, not the screen. Neither was the complete answer she wanted.","\"The absence of a clear image doesn't establish that I entered twelve,\" she said. \"Nor does it establish that I entered twenty-four. Can the interface retain its input history?\"","The technician nodded reluctantly. He was instructed to preserve it before making any adjustment. The controller was sealed, and the demonstration ended. There would be no applause for a recovered miracle. There would be a decision based on what she had actually done.","Offstage, the teacher from the charter school gave her a paper cup of water. \"You know, I used to tell my class to admit when they didn't know. I found it more difficult when the head was watching.\"","\"Did you manage?\"","\"Eventually. Usually after saying something very silly first.\"","Mara drank. The kindness helped the dread ebb. She had not been asked to starve for the sake of intellectual pride, endure an arbitrary humiliation, or prove her individual worth by never failing. She had been asked to make a choice about a fragile object. She had chosen care. Even if she lost, that part could remain hers.","The scores arrived an hour later. She lost points for an incomplete demonstration and gained them for stopping before further damage occurred. The judges described her response as competent and her conclusions as appropriately limited. Her total was the lowest of those admitted to the next round.","\"I have passed on a razor edge,\" she said.","Cassian read the sheet. \"You passed while refusing to pretend. That may be the more sustainable method.\"","\"You make defeat sound extravagant.\"","\"It isn't defeat. And I am told accuracy matters.\"","She looked at his hands. A little cream still marked one cuff. He had made no attempt to shift the blame, enlist his title, or speak on her behalf. His presence had been a quiet fact at the edge of her work, supporting without occupying it.","\"Thank you,\" she said.","\"For switching it off?\"","\"For waiting until I asked.\"","He nodded once. It was enough.","At the workshop, Holt set a sealed envelope beside the theater. Inside was the quarterly maintenance note for the shared controller. Its last sentence stated that a calibration error could double an imported speed value. Beneath it, a different hand had written: \"Resolved. Do not circulate.\"","The repair date was blank."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"Why does Mara refuse to restart the demonstration immediately after the mechanism speeds
